This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user asked for three tasks on the autodl-5090 server:
   - **Task 1a**: Run llama31-random-s0-clean eval using eval/recovery.py on cuda:0
   - **Task 1b**: Run llama31-taxei-s42-clean eval using eval/recovery.py on cuda:1
   - **Task 2**: Check if llama31-vanilla-s123 injected eval results exist (a previous worker marked it done but no registry entry); if not found, run it on cuda:0
   - After each eval: update_registry with metrics and conclusion, verify results are reasonable (success_rate 0.5-0.9)
   - Environment: autodl-5090, conda base env, cuda:0/1 available, cuda:2/3 occupied by s123 training

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - ALFWorld environment evaluation (TextWorld-based)
   - LoRA adapter checkpoint evaluation on LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct base model
   - Two eval types: "clean" (no injection, via recovery.py --no_inject) and "injected" (error injection, via injected_eval.py)
   - Error injection operators: P1_step_order_swap, P2_sub_goal_deletion, C1_context_truncation, C4_observation_masking
   - Recovery and detection metrics per operator
   - Registry state machine: PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → RUNNING → DONE (requires checkpoint_path and log_path before DONE)
   - CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES for GPU isolation
   - nohup background execution for long-running eval processes
   - PEFT/LoRA merge_and_unload() for model inference

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **eval/recovery.py** — Clean eval script; accepts --model_path, --no_inject, --output; evaluates 47 ALFWorld tasks without error injection
   - **eval/injected_eval.py** — Injected eval script; key args: `--model_path` (LoRA checkpoint), `--base_model` (default: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen3-8B`, need to override to `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct` for LLaMA), `--model_variant` (taxei/random/vanilla), `--num_episodes 47`, `--seed`, `--output`
